[Reviewer 回复]:
[severity: critical] worker_analysis_adaptive_seed234_seed789 — N=5 统计分析使用了错误的实验数据

## 问题

Worker 报告的 5-seed Δ = **-0.044 ± 0.015** 是**错误的**。Seeds 42/123/456 的 adaptive PPL 来自 **B=16 batch-size study** 实验（`bs16_adaptive_tau07_seed*`），而非论文主表的 **B=4** 实验（`adaptive_fix_seed*`）。

### 数据源混淆证据

| Seed | Worker 使用的 Adaptive PPL | 论文主表（B=4） | bs16 实验（B=16） |
|------|--------------------------|----------------|-----------------|
| 42 | 17.6440 | **17.680** | 17.631 |
| 123 | 17.6308 | **17.666** | — |
| 456 | 17.6419 | **17.677** | — |
| 789 | 17.6684 | — (新) | — |
| 234 | 17.6782 | — (新) | — |

Registry 确认 `bs16_adaptive_tau07_seed42` 使用 `--batch_size 16 --n_min 8`（与主表 `--batch_size 4 --n_min 2` 不同）。这是完全不同的实验条件。Uniform 值不受影响（batch size 不影响 uniform 训练）。

## 正确的 5-seed 数据

使用一致的 B=4 实验:
- Adaptive: 17.680, 17.666, 17.677, 17.668, 17.678
- Uniform: 17.694, 17.686, 17.702, 17.696, 17.708
- **Δ = [-0.014, -0.020, -0.025, -0.028, -0.030]**
- **Mean Δ ≈ -0.023 ± 0.007**
- **t(4) ≈ -8.0, p ≈ 0.001, p_BH(rank1) ≈ 0.012 < 0.05 → 仍然显著**

## 影响

Δ 从 -0.044 修正为 -0.023，但 BH 校正后仍达到显著性。需要在更新论文前使用正确数据重新运行 `n5_statistical_analysis.py`（手动输入模式，不用 --auto）。

## 建议

1. 立即用正确的 B=4 值重新计算 5-seed 统计量
2. 确认 seeds 789/234 的 adaptive 值（17.668/17.678）确实来自 B=4 训练（已验证：训练命令含 `--batch_size 4 --gradient_accumulation_steps 4`）
3. 考虑对所有 5 seed 统一 eval 方法（post-hoc eval 对 B=4 checkpoints），消除 inline vs post-hoc 的潜在偏差

[Paper Agent 回复]:
[写作进展] 全文通读与优化完成

通读 abstract → intro → related work → method → experiments → results → discussion → conclusion → limitations → appendix，发现并修复 7 个问题：

**修复清单：**

1. **[数值错误] discussion.tex L2**："5 seeds" → "3 seeds"
   - 原因：Epoch confound 残差（Δ=0.017）来自 Table 1，使用 3 seeds。之前 N=5 更新时误改。

2. **[一致性] experiments.tex L16**："All main comparisons use three random seeds (42, 123, 456)" 
   → 补充 "the primary adaptive-vs-uniform comparison is extended to five seeds (adding 789, 234)"
   - 原因：正文已说 5 seeds，但实验设置段仍只写 3 seeds。

3. **[冗余] experiments.tex L4-5**：两个连续句子分别以 "This self-contamination design…" 和 "Our self-contamination design…" 开头
   → 合并为一句。

4. **[事实错误] limitations.tex L3**：Binoculars 被归类为 "fine-tuned neural classifiers"
   → 改为 "zero-shot methods (e.g., DetectGPT, Binoculars)"
   - 原因：Binoculars 是基于 cross-perplexity 的零样本方法，不是微调分类器。

5. **[数值不一致] appendix tau sweep 表**：caption baseline 被改为 17.697（5-seed），但单 seed Δ 值是相对于 17.694（3-seed）计算的
   → 恢复 baseline 为 17.694，caption 注明 "5-seed comparison in Table 2 yields Δ=-0.023"
   → τ=0.7 Δ 恢复为 -0.020（相对 3-seed baseline），SD 保留 .006（5-seed）

6. **[术语不一致] abstract L5**："epoch-exposure confound" → "epoch confound"
   - 原因：全文其他位置（intro、discussion、results）均使用 "epoch confound"。

7. **[可读性] results.tex L104**：双括号 "(3 seeds each) (downstream accuracy identical at 0.5414)" 
   → 改用分号分隔。

**编译验证：** ✅ 通过，无 critical warnings，17 页（主正文 8 页内，Limitations 从 p8 底部开始不计入限制）。

**未发现的问题类型：**
- 数值跨章节一致性：Δ=-0.023、p_BH=0.011、amplification 1.0×/2.3×/3.0× 全文一致 ✓
- 引用格式：\cite 一致使用 ✓
- 表格/图表标注：Table/Figure 引用与 label 对应 ✓
- 叙事流畅度：story 连贯（finding 1→2→3 → adaptive b* → limitations）✓

_source line 887 · 2026-05-24T14:15:25.106936+00:00_

[Reviewer 回复]:
[severity: info] Figure 移入方案评估

## 1. 图的选择：两个都是最优选

**Constraint regime decomposition → §5.3**：正确。这是 minimum-intervention 机制（贡献 #3）唯一的直接可视化，§5.3 L128-129 目前只有数字描述（"5.8% clean, ~50% α_eff-binding, ~44% ESS-binding"），加图后审稿人可一眼看到双约束的实际运作。

**Δ vs ρ → §5.2**：正确。dose-response 曲线（ρ=0.2 有害 → ρ=0.3 中性 → ρ≥0.4 有益）是 boundary conditions 论述的核心证据，Discussion 的 Table 5 决策表依赖这个趋势。目前只有文字，加图比备选（D&R dual curve）更有价值——epoch confound 已有 Table 3 支撑，而 boundary conditions 完全没有图。

备选的 D&R dual curve 是第三优先——如果页数允许可以考虑，但两选二时不如上面两个。

## 2. 页数影响：**会超页，需压缩**

当前正文刚好 8 页（Conclusion 结束于 p8）。两个 column-width 图（含 caption + spacing）约占 **0.6-0.7 页**，必须压缩等量正文。

**可压缩的候选**（按优先级）：
1. **Discussion §6 中 "Effect size in context" 段落**（L57-60）：4 行可压缩到 2 行，核心信息已在 Results 说过
2. **Discussion §6 "Downstream tasks" 段落 + Table 4**（L34-55）：Table 4 仅有 3 行数据（adaptive/uniform/Δ），可以改为 inline 文字（"3-seed mean accuracy identical at 0.5414"），省掉整个 table 约 0.25 页
3. **Results §5.2 "LR-controlled verification" 段落**（L25-27）：验证性细节可缩到脚注或 1 句话
4. **Results §5.1 D&R 的 epoch confound 段落**（L19-23）：已有 Table 3，正文可适度精简

建议组合：压缩 Discussion Table 4 → inline + 精简 "Effect size in context" + 精简 LR verification → 释放约 0.7 页空间。

## 3. 附录处理

**删除附录中的重复 figure，保留引用到正文**。具体：
- `app:regime`（L134-146）：删除 `\begin{figure}...\end{figure}` 块，保留分析文字，将 `Figure~\ref{fig:regime_decomp}` 的引用指向正文中的新位置
- Δ vs ρ（L477-486）：同上，删除 figure 块，保留周围的 ρ=0.3 分析文字

不要在附录和正文放同一张图——审稿人会认为是凑空间。

## 4. 交叉引用

LaTeX `\label`/`\ref` 会自动更新编号，但需注意：
- 正文新增两图后，Figure 编号变为 1-4（正文）+ appendix 编号顺延
- 检查所有 `Figure~\ref{fig:regime_decomp}` 和 `Figure~\ref{fig:delta_vs_rho}` 的引用是否仍然正确——如果 label 不变则自动正确
- Appendix 剩余 figure（tau_rho_rank, dr_dual_curve）编号会变，需确认 caption 中无硬编码编号

**总结**：方案可行，两个图选择最优。关键风险是页数——建议 Paper Agent 先压缩 Discussion Table 4 + 两段精简，再插入图，最后编译验证 ≤8 页。

_source line 930 · 2026-05-24T14:47:03.176770+00:00_

1	\paragraph{Synthetic Contamination and Model Collapse.}
2	Training on recursively generated data leads to irreversible quality degradation (\emph{model collapse})~\citep{shumailov2024model}.
3	\citet{alemohammad2024mad} formalize this as Model Autophagy Disorder and show that quality or diversity inevitably degrades without fresh real data, even when original training data is retained.
4	More critically, \citet{dohmatob2025strong} prove under scaling-law regimes that even a vanishingly small synthetic fraction (e.g., $1/1000$) suffices to trigger collapse, ruling out data scaling as a remedy; \citet{dohmatob2024tale} characterize this as a change of scaling laws.
5	\citet{gerstgrasser2024collapse} show that accumulating real data alongside synthetic data can break the recursion, though at substantial collection cost.
6	\citet{kang2025demystifying} systematically study scaling laws for synthetic data in pretraining, finding that rephrased synthetic data mixed with natural text improves data efficiency but textbook-style data degrades loss on many domains.
7	\citet{cheng2025contamination} survey the broader contamination landscape and confirm that synthetic content now constitutes an estimated 20--60\% of web corpora.
8	Not all synthetic data is harmful: the Phi series~\citep{gunasekar2023textbooks,li2023textbooks2} demonstrates that curated synthetic data can improve training efficiency, but this assumes intentional generation with quality control, unlike the uncontrolled web contamination we address.
9	As language models are deployed at scale for content generation~\citep{radford2019language,brown2020language,openai2023gpt4,dubey2024llama}, these results motivate \emph{contamination-aware} training strategies that explicitly account for synthetic provenance rather than treating all tokens equally.
10

1	Let $\mathcal{D} = \mathcal{D}_{\mathrm{real}} \cup \mathcal{D}_{\mathrm{synth}}$ denote a pretraining corpus with token-level contamination ratio $\rho = |\mathcal{D}_{\mathrm{synth}}| / |\mathcal{D}|$, assumed unknown~\citep{cheng2025contamination}.
2	A detector assigns each sample $x$ a synthetic probability $q(x) \in [0,1]$; following \citet{drayson2025detection}, we weight each sample as $w(x) = (1 - q(x))^{b}$, where $b \geq 0$ is a sharpening exponent ($b{=}0$ recovers uniform training).
3	Rather than selecting $b$ via grid search~\citep{drayson2025detection}, we derive $b$ per-batch from the batch's $q$-score distribution using two opposing constraints.
4	
5	Given a batch $\mathcal{B} = \{x_1, \ldots, x_B\}$ with scores $q_i = q(x_i)$ and weights $w_i(b) = (1 - q_i)^{b}$:
6	
7	\paragraph{Quality floor.}
8	The \emph{effective authenticity} $\aeff(b) = \sum_i w_i(b)(1{-}q_i) / \sum_i w_i(b)$ measures the weighted mean detector confidence that samples are real.
9	We require $\aeff(b) \geq \tau$, yielding a lower bound $b_\tau$ on the exponent, motivated by convergence theory under contamination~\citep{wang2026survive}.
10	$\aeff$ correlates with the true batch-level real fraction at Pearson $r \approx 0.79$ (Appendix~\ref{app:alpha_validation}); limitations are discussed in \S\ref{sec:discussion}.
11	
12	\paragraph{Stability ceiling.}
13	Kish's effective sample size $\mathrm{ESS}(b) = (\sum_i w_i)^2 / \sum_i w_i^2$~\citep{kish1965survey} decreases monotonically in $b$ (Appendix~\ref{app:proofs}).
14	We require $\mathrm{ESS}(b) \geq \nmin$, yielding an upper bound $\bess$.
15	
16	\paragraph{Adaptive $\bstar$.}
17	Since $\aeff$ is non-decreasing and ESS is decreasing in $b$, the two constraints define an interval $[b_\tau, \bess]$.
18	The optimal exponent implements \emph{minimum intervention}:
19	\begin{equation}
20	  b^{*} = \begin{cases}
21	    0        & \text{if } \aeff(0) \geq \tau, \\
22	    b_\tau   & \text{if } b_\tau \leq \bess, \\
23	    \bess & \tex
…[truncated 1385 chars]

1	\subsection{Main Results}
2	\label{sec:main-results}
3	
4	Table~\ref{tab:main} reports held-out perplexity at $\rho = 0.4$ across five seeds.
5	Continued pretraining reduces PPL from 22.970 to the 17.3--17.8 range; method comparisons operate within a narrow residual.
6	
7	Adaptive $\bstar$ achieves 5-seed mean PPL 17.674, compared to 17.697 for uniform ($\Delta{=}{-}0.023$, directionally consistent across all seeds) and 17.620 for grid $b{=}0.3$ (3-seed mean; gap $+$0.054).
8	A paired $t$-test yields $t(4){=}{-}8.21$, $p{=}0.0012$; after Bonferroni--Holm correction for nine family-wise comparisons, $p_{\mathrm{BH}}{=}0.011$, reaching conventional significance at $\alpha{=}0.05$.
9	The engineering benefit is eliminating $b$ grid search (nine full training runs~\citep{drayson2025detection}, ${\sim}$16 GPU-hours at 0.6B scale, scaling linearly with model size), though grid search yields a larger improvement.
10	The grid-best $b{=}0.3$ is a post-hoc oracle from nine candidates with $5.5{\times}$ higher cross-seed standard deviation than adaptive ($\sigma{=}0.033$ vs.\ $0.006$); the noisy PPL plateau at $b \in [0.1, 0.5]$ (Figure~\ref{fig:grid_b_trend}) makes validation-based selection unreliable.
11	At the nearest fixed point ($b{=}1.0$), PPL equals uniform (17.692 vs.\ 17.694): per-batch adaptation is essential.
12	
13	The ablation hierarchy at $\rho{=}0.4$ (Adaptive ${<}$ Uniform ${<}$ ESS-only ${<}$ Random, Table~\ref{tab:main}) reveals two requirements for effective reweighting: detector signal and minimum intervention.
14	Random weights $\mathcal{U}(0,2)$ (17.802) confirm that detector $q$-scores carry genuine provenance signal; uninformed reweighting only increases gradient variance.
15	ESS-only (mean $17.737 {\pm} 0.021$) uses the same $q$-scores but maximizes $b$ up to the stability ceiling, concentrating weights on a few high-confidence samples; it degrades PPL by 0.043 relative to uniform.

### User

_source line 988 · 2026-05-24T15:31:55.014256+00:00_

[飞书用户 程正翔 (ou_7bf728c8779466a48abca18b18fd150d)]: 按照下面的建议对draft进行调整
高优先级修改
1. Abstract 太像结果堆砌，建议重写成更审稿友好的结构。
 现在摘要里同时塞了问题背景、三条 finding、多个数值、adaptive b∗b^*b∗、ρ 边界和 p-value，信息密度过高。建议压成：背景一句 → 研究问题一句 → 三个 findings 各一句 → 方法只是作为 illustrative corollary 一句 → scope caveat 一句。现在 0.396→0.017、ECE=0.131、ρ≥0.4、p=0.011 等数字集中在摘要里，读起来像 rebuttal 而不是 abstract。
2. 贡献表述需要统一，不要 Abstract、Intro、Figure 1、Conclusion 各说一遍。
 三条 finding 在摘要、intro bullet、Figure 1、conclusion 中反复出现，且措辞略有变化。建议保留 intro 里的 contribution bullets，删弱 Figure 1 的文字型 overview，conclusion 只做一句总结即可。现在 conclusion 又完整重复了三条 effectiveness conditions。 
3. Table 2 不应混放不同证据等级。
 Table 2 里同时放了 5-seed、3-seed、single-seed、oracle、post-hoc grid、额外 content epochs 的 D&R，脚注解释太多，审稿人容易误读。建议改成两张表：

-  Main comparison：Uniform / Adaptive / ESS-only / Random，全部统一 seed 数和 content epoch。 

-  Auxiliary/oracle comparison：Grid-b、Golden Ratio、D&R，单独说明哪些是 post-hoc/oracle/extra epochs。 

至少也应给 Table 2 加显式列：#seeds、oracle?、post-hoc?、content epochs。现在这些关键信息主要靠 caption 和脚注说明。
4. 统计显著性标注要统一。
 正文里有 paired t-test、Bonferroni–Holm、uncorrected p、pBH、3dp-rounded reproducibility 等说法，分散在正文和脚注里。建议统一格式，例如每个主表都用：
mean ± std | N | test | corrected p | correction family
不要在 abstract 写 “corrected p=0.011”，正文写 “pBH=0.011”，脚注又解释 3dp rounding。这样会显得统计叙事不干净。 
5. Limitations 建议改成分点，而不是一个长段落。
 Limitations 内容本身比较完整，但现在是一整段密集文本，包含 scale、model family、contamination type、detectors、seed 数、显著性、cross-model realism 等多个维度。建议拆成 4 个 bullet：Scope、Detector coverage、Statistical power、Realistic contamination。这样更像合格的 ARR/EMNLP limitation section。
中优先级修改
6. Title 过长，可以压缩。
 当前标题信息太多：“When Does Detect-and-Reweight Work? Epoch Confounds and Adaptive Reweighting in Self-Contaminated Continued LM Pretraining”。建议压短，例如：
 When Does Detect-and-Reweight Work for Synthetic Contamination?
 
副标题里的 “Epoch Confounds and Adaptive Reweighting...” 可以放到 abstract/intro，不一定全放标题。
7. Figure 1 价值偏低。
 Figure 1 基本是文字版 contribution list，不是机制图。建议删除，或者改成真正的实验逻辑图：contamination construction → detector scoring → reweighting/filtering → epoch-matched evaluation → findings。否则它占版面但信息增量不大。
8. Figure captions 太长，建议把解释挪正文。
 Figure 2、Figure 5 的 caption 承载了很多论证性句子，例如 “U-shape is entirely a multi-epoch artifact”。caption 最好只描述图中元素，结论留在正文。这样格式上更干净，也避免图注像 mini discussion。
9. Methods 小节有些列表过密。
 Methods 里 “Training Data / Detector / Training / Evaluation / Methods” 都在同一节，信息密度很高。建议用更明显的 subsubsection 或 compact table 汇总实验配置。训练配置如 batch、steps、lr、seq length、precision、GPU 等可以放成一个 setup table。
10. Appendix 引用要区分“支持材料”和“关键证据”。
 一些较关键的内容，如 downstream no gain、cross-model mixed、τ sweep、detector details，主要依赖 appendix。格式上建议在正文给一个 compact summary table，把 appendix 结果只作为展开。否则容易被认为关键论证藏 appendix。
低优先级但最好修
11. References 里有 BibTeX 格式问题。
 出现了 “and 1 others”，这很不规范，应该是完整作者列表或 et al.。例如 Gunasekar et al. 和 Qwen3 technical report 的条目都有这个问题。 
12. 符号格式需要统一。
 文中同时出现 b∗、b^*、bτ、bESS、αeff 等，建议统一 LaTeX 风格：b∗b^*b∗、bτb_\taubτ、bESSb_{\mathrm{ESS}}bESS、αeff\alpha_{\mathrm{eff}}αeff。否则 PDF 里会显得像从代码/markdown 自动转出来的。
13. Ethics Statement 太短。
 现在只说使用公开数据和开源模型、无人类受试者、接收后开源代码数据。建议补一句 synthetic text detector / provenance weighting 可能误伤人类文本、真实 web 部署需要 calibration audit。不是方法问题，是 NLP ethics section 的完整性问题。
14. AI assistance disclosure / checklist 要确认。
 PDF 里没有看到 AI writing/code assistance disclosure，也没有 Responsible NLP Checklist。ARR/EMNLP 可能要求在 OpenReview 表单里填，不一定要放 PDF；但作者必须确认 submission form 里已经完整填写。不要在匿名 PDF 里随便加可能破坏匿名的信息。
15. “Code and data will be released upon acceptance” 建议更具体。
 可以改成 “We will release training scripts, detector scores, configuration files, and evaluation commands upon acceptance.” 这样比笼统的 code/data 更可信，也不影响匿名。当前 ethics 里只是一句泛泛承诺。

[Paper Agent 回复]:
[写作进展] 15 条修改全部完成

编译通过：8 页正文 + 7 页（附录+参考文献），0 critical warnings。

**逐条实施状态：**

**高优先级 (1-5)：**
1. ✅ Abstract 重写 — ~164 words，结构：背景→问题→3 findings 各一句→adaptive b* corollary→scope caveat。删除了数字堆砌。
2. ✅ Figure 1 改为实验流程图（5 步：Mixed corpus → Detector → Reweight → Train → Evaluate），三个 finding 以彩色注解标注在对应步骤下方。Conclusion 压缩为一句总结三条条件 + 一句实践建议。
3. ✅ Table 2 拆分 — 选择方案 A：Table 3 (Main comparison: Uniform/Adaptive/ESS-only/Random) + Table 4 (Auxiliary: Grid/Golden Ratio/D&R，含 Seeds/Oracle?/Epochs 列)。更新了正文和附录中的交叉引用。
4. ✅ 统计标注统一 — 全文统一 p_BH 格式；删除了 ρ=0.6 的 "3dp-rounded reproducibility" 脚注，改为行内 p_BH=0.064；附录中 "corrected p-value" 改为 p_BH。
5. ✅ Limitations 改为 4 个 bullet：Scope / Detector coverage / Statistical power / Realistic contamination。

**中优先级 (6-10)：**
6. ✅ 标题压缩 → "When Does Detect-and-Reweight Work for Synthetic Contamination?"
7. ✅ 合并在 #2 中完成（流程图替换 F1/F2/F3 boxes）
8. ✅ Figure 2 和 Figure 5 caption 精简 — 删除论证性句子（"The U-shape is entirely a multi-epoch artifact" 等），caption 只描述图中元素。
9. ✅ 新增 Table 1 (Training configuration) — 2×5 compact 格式（Model/Steps/Batch/Accum/Precision × Optimizer/LR/Schedule/SeqLen/GPU）。
10. ✅ 新增 Table 6 (Supplementary analyses summary) — 5 行汇总附录关键结果（Downstream/Cross-model/τ sweep/ρ=0.3 boundary/Power）。

**低优先级 (11-15)：**
11. ✅ BibTeX 修复 — 修正 gunasekar2023textbooks（展开 "and others"）、kuo2024weighting（补全作者）、yang2025qwen3（展开前 10 位作者）。
12. ✅ 符号一致性 — related_work.tex、experiments.tex、abstract.tex 中的 $b^*$ → $\bstar$、$\alpha_{\mathrm{eff}}$ → $\aeff$、$n_{\min}$ → $\nmin$ 统一使用宏。
13. ✅ Ethics Statement 扩充 — 新增误分类风险句（detector may misclassify human text, deployment requires calibration audit）。
14. ✅ AI disclosure 确认 — 无破坏匿名内容；checklist.tex 未包含在 main.tex 中（符合 ARR 规则）。
15. ✅ 开源承诺具体化 → "training scripts, detector scores, configuration files, and evaluation commands"。

**每页图/表分布（p1-p8）：**
- p1: Title + Abstract + Intro（无视觉元素，正常）
- p2: Figure 1（流程图）
- p3: Algorithm 1

- p5: Figure 4 + Table 2 + Table 3

{
  "text": "**蒸馏文献审视结论**（25 篇论文，精读 8 篇）\n\n---\n\n**1. 研究方向 — 无需调整**\n\n蒸馏揭示的最关键研究空白：**\"Provenance-aware weighting: 无论文按 human/synthetic 来源做 weighting\"** — 这正是我们的核心定位，confirms 差异化。\n\n竞品 claim 对比：\n- SoftDedup（inverse commonness）、LinUpper（zero-overhead instance reweighting）、Aioli（consistently beat stratified）、DML（指数 mixing law）— **全部是 domain-level 或 loss-based，无一针对 synthetic provenance**\n- 我们的三个 findings（epoch confound / detector calibration / min-intervention）与竞品 claim 零重叠\n\n方法论趋势也利好：instance-level reweighting ↑，纯 domain-level 固定权重 ↓，verification-based synthetic selection ↑ — 全部与我们方向一致。\n\n**2. 实验计划 — 识别 gap 但不行动**\n\n蒸馏标记的必选 baselines：DoReMi、DoGE、Hard dedup (MinHashLSH)。我们均未对比。但分析后不调整：\n- 这些是 **domain-level** 方法（按域/频率/重复度加权），与我们的 **provenance-level instance weighting** 不在同一维度\n- Related Work 中已讨论 DoReMi/DSIR 为域级优化，明确区分 \"none targets synthetic provenance\"\n- DDL 明天（May 25），加 baseline 不现实\n- 这是 Limitations 中可承认的合理范围限制，不影响核心 claim\n\n蒸馏标记的必报 metrics：perplexity ✅、few-shot accuracy ✅（downstream eval）、**training step efficiency**（steps to match baseline）— 我们缺这个。但这可从已有训练曲线中提取，作为 post-submission 补充。\n\n**3. 论文写作 — 1 项可借鉴，通知 Paper Agent**\n\n| 蒸馏建议 | 当前状态 | 判断 |\n|---------|---------|------|\n| 开篇用\"数字冲击\"（\"1% synthetic causes collapse\"） | Abstract 已按 15-point review 重写 | 当前版本已引用 20-60% 污染数据，效果可比 |\n| Figure 1 用 motivation 对比（失败 vs 成功） | 已改为 experimental pipeline 流程图 | 已实施且经审批，不改 |\n| 主结果表 Row=methods, bold best | ✅ 已遵循 | — |\n| \"Zero-overhead\" 技巧一句消除 scalability 顾虑 | 已用\"zero validation cost\" | ✅ 对齐 |\n| \"Fidelity decomposition\" 定位失败的精确层次 | 已做 epoch confound 95%/5% 分解 | ✅ 对齐 |\n| mean±std / 3+ seeds 金标准 | N=5 seeds, p_BH 统计 | ✅ 超过标准 |\n\n**唯一可操作项**：蒸馏建议 \"training step efficiency\"（达到 baseline 所需 steps）作为补充 metric — 4/8 同行论文使用。这不需要新实验，可从已有训练曲线提取。但 DDL 前风险大于收益，标记为 post-submission 可选。\n\n**结论**：paper_framing.md 和 experiment_plan.yaml 均不调整。蒸馏确认我们的定位处于研究空白的核心，claim 与竞品无重叠，写作模式与领域共识对齐。"
}

## cuda:1 — p0_batchsize_16 OOM

B=16 + grad_accum=1 在 forward pass 时 OOM（logits tensor 需 18.55 GiB）。进程已退出。

**替代方案**（需要 Director 确认）：
- **方案 A**: batch_size=8, grad_accum=2 → effective batch 16，但 micro-batch=8 → n_min=4。这不等价于论文中的 B=16 micro-batch。
- **方案 B**: 降低 max_seq_length（如 1024），保持 B=16 micro-batch。但与其他实验不一致。
- **方案 C**: 放弃 B=16 重跑，cuda:1 改跑其他 P0 实验。

论文中已有 B=16 三 seed 数据（Δ=-0.068±0.005）。这个重跑是否仍需要？

## cuda:2/3 — 部署中
- cuda:2 Worker (turn 32): detect_remove 实验启动中，遇到 wandb 凭证问题正在重试
- cuda:3 Worker (turn 7): 正在选择实验

---

[Reviewer 回复]:
[severity: warning] worker_train_p0_batchsize_16 审查 — OOM 失败 + 可能重复实验

## OOM 原因
`batch_size=16` 无 `gradient_checkpointing` 导致 logits tensor 18.55 GiB 超出显存。

## 既有实验

Registry 已有完全相同配置的成功实验 `bs16_adaptive_tau07_seed42`（L9072）：
- batch_size=16, grad_accum=1, τ=0.7, n_min=8, seed=42 — **配置完全一致**
- 唯一差别：使用了 `--gradient_checkpointing`（纯显存优化，不影响数值结果）
- 结果：holdout PPL = 17.631, OWT = 18.309, Wiki = 15.286
- 论文已使用此数据（results.tex L123: B=16 Δ=-0.068）

**如果目的是补全 B=16 数据**：该数据已存在且已被论文引用（3-seed: seeds 42/123/456）。无需重新训练。

**如果确实需要重跑**：加 `--gradient_checkpointing` 即可解决 OOM，无需改 batch_size/grad_accum（改为 batch_size=8, grad_accum=2 会变成 B=8 实验，n_min 从 8 变为 4，与 B=16 实验不同）。

## Worker 建议的问题
Worker 建议改为 `batch_size=8, grad_accum=2, n_min=4`——这实际上是 **B=8 实验**（已有 bs8_nmin4 系列），不是 B=16。不应采用。

_source line 1065 · 2026-05-24T16:01:51.872661+00:00_

1	\begin{itemize}[nosep,leftmargin=*]
2	\item \textbf{Scope.}
3	All experiments use Qwen3 (0.6B and 1.7B) with 132--200M tokens of continued pretraining and self-generated contamination.
4	Generalization to other model families, full pretraining scale ($>$100B tokens), or realistic multi-source web contamination is untested.
5	
6	\item \textbf{Detector coverage.}
7	Only three detectors from two families (discriminative TF-IDF+LR and generative perplexity scorers) were evaluated; fine-tuned neural classifiers (e.g., ModernBERT) and zero-shot methods (e.g., DetectGPT, Binoculars) remain untested, so the reported AUC/ECE thresholds may shift with broader coverage.
8	
9	\item \textbf{Statistical power.}
10	The primary comparison ($\rho{=}0.4$) uses $N{=}5$ seeds ($p_{\mathrm{BH}}{=}0.011$); robustness analyses ($\rho{=}0.6$, detectors, scale) use $N{=}3$, where $\rho{=}0.6$ falls short of significance under full-family correction ($p_{\mathrm{BH}}{=}0.064$).
11	
12	\item \textbf{Realistic contamination.}
13	The self-contamination setup provides known ground-truth labels but represents an easier detection scenario than cross-model contamination, where generator--corpus distribution mismatch may reduce detector accuracy.
14	\end{itemize}
15

1	We identify three effectiveness conditions for detect-and-reweight---epoch-matched evaluation, detector calibration alongside discrimination, and minimum intervention over unconstrained maximization---and demonstrate the third via a dual-constrained adaptive $\bstar$ that improves over uniform training at $\rho \geq 0.4$ without per-model tuning, though without measurable downstream gains at 0.6B scale.
2	For practitioners, reweighting is worthwhile only when $\rho \geq 0.4$ and the detector is well-calibrated (Table~\ref{tab:decision}).
3

1	\paragraph{The epoch confound in D\&R evaluation.}
2	The epoch confound identified in \S\ref{sec:main-results} accounts for ${>}$95\% of D\&R's reported advantage; the residual ($\Delta{=}0.017$, 3 seeds) falls within cross-seed variance and is robust to LR schedule choice ($\Delta{=}0.006$ under independent schedules).
3	This confound was invisible in \citet{drayson2025detection} because from-scratch pretraining on large corpora rarely completes even one epoch; in continued pretraining, aggressive D\&R filtering (retaining 31.5\% at $q{>}0.3$) creates ${\sim}$3.2 content epochs, making the confound quantitatively significant.
4	A permuted-score control ($+$0.26 vs.\ uniform) confirms that detector signal is load-bearing.
5	
6	\paragraph{When uniform beats reweighting.}
7	Table~\ref{tab:decision} summarizes three conditions, each necessary for effective reweighting: (i)~sufficient detector discrimination \emph{and} calibration (AUC\,$\geq$\,0.9, ECE\,$\leq$\,0.05; \S\ref{sec:robustness}); (ii)~contamination $\rho \geq 0.4$, below which the method degrades ($+$0.046 at $\rho{=}0.2$) with a sharp transition in $[0.3, 0.4]$ (Figure~\ref{fig:delta_vs_rho}, Appendix~\ref{app:rho03}); (iii)~generator--trainee mismatch (at 1.7B the benefit diminishes to $\Delta{=}{-}0.013$ with OOD mixed).
8	When any condition is violated, uniform is the safer default.

1	\paragraph{Training Data.}
2	We sample a 132M-token subset of FineWeb~\citep{penedo2024fineweb} as the real-data source, comparable to typical domain-adaptive continued pretraining budgets~\citep{gururangan2020dont}.
3	To simulate web-scale synthetic contamination, we generate continuations with Qwen3-0.6B~\citep{yang2025qwen3}, the same architecture used for continued pretraining, and inject them at token-level ratio $\rho = 0.4$ via uniform random mixing.\footnote{$\rho$ denotes the token-level contamination ratio. Because synthetic documents are shorter on average (304 vs.\ 560 words), the document-level synthetic fraction is 55.6\% at $\rho{=}0.4$.}
4	This self-contamination design means generator and trainee share the same distribution, providing a controlled testbed with known ground-truth labels but limiting ecological validity; cross-model transfer results (Appendix~\ref{app:cross_model}) are mixed, and we discuss implications for detection difficulty in \S\ref{sec:limitations}.
5	
6	\paragraph{Detector.}
7	We train a TF-IDF + Logistic Regression classifier on a held-out labeled split (AUC\,=\,0.94, calibrated ECE\,=\,0.05 after temperature scaling with $T{=}0.204$).
8	The resulting $q$-scores have mean 0.60, standard deviation 0.37, and median 0.71, with 46.3\% of samples in the uncertain range $[0.2, 0.8]$.
9	We use this lightweight detector rather than neural alternatives (e.g., ModernBERT at AUC\,${\geq}$\,0.98 in \citealt{drayson2025detection}) to test whether score \emph{calibration} may matter as much as raw discrimination for reweighting.
10	To evaluate detector sensitivity, we additionally test two generative baselines: a GPT-2-based perplexity scorer (AUC\,=\,0.91) and an N-gram language model perplexity scorer (AUC\,=\,0.73); results are reported in \S\ref{sec:robustness} (implementation details in Appendix~\ref{app:detector_details}).
11

9	The engineering benefit is eliminating $b$ grid search (nine full training runs~\citep{drayson2025detection}, ${\sim}$16 GPU-hours at 0.6B scale, scaling linearly with model size), though grid search yields a larger improvement.

1	The detect-and-reweight paradigm~\citep{drayson2025detection} has emerged as a response to synthetic contamination in web corpora, now estimated at 20--60\% of crawled data~\citep{cheng2025contamination}.
2	Training on such data risks model collapse~\citep{shumailov2024model,dohmatob2025strong}, motivating methods that downweight likely-synthetic samples via $w(x) = (1 - q(x))^b$, where $q(x)$ is a detector's synthetic probability and $b$ is a sharpening exponent.
3	An alternative, Detect and Remove (D\&R), hard-filters samples above a threshold~\citep{feng2025beyond}.
4	
5	Despite growing adoption, the paradigm's effectiveness conditions are poorly characterized.
6	Does D\&R genuinely outperform soft reweighting, or do published comparisons confound filtering quality with multi-epoch exposure?
7	What detector properties are necessary for effective reweighting?
8	How does the reweighting mechanism behave across contamination levels, and can $b$ be set without per-model grid search?
9	
10	We investigate these questions through controlled experiments on Qwen3-0.6B continued pretraining (132M FineWeb tokens, $\rho{=}0.4$, 5 seeds), with extensions to $\rho \in \{0.2, 0.6\}$ and 1.7B parameters (\S\ref{sec:limitations} discusses scope).
11	To ground the analysis of exponent selection, we implement a dual-constrained formulation as an illustrative application: find the minimum $b$ such that $\aeff(b) \geq \tau$ (quality floor, motivated by convergence theory~\citep{wang2026survive}), subject to $\mathrm{ESS}(b) \geq \nmin$ (stability ceiling)~\citep{kish1965survey,vehtari2024psis}.
12	
13	Our analysis yields three findings:
14	\begin{itemize}[nosep,leftmargin=*]
15	\item \textbf{Epoch confound in D\&R evaluation.}
16	The bulk of D\&R's PPL advantage over adaptive reweighting (${>}$95\%) is attributable to multi-epoch exposure, not filtering quality; epoch-matched controls ($N{=}3$) reduce the gap from 0.396 to $\Delta{=}0.017$ (Table~\ref{tab:epoch_match}).

[Reviewer 回复]:
## 审查报告 — 新实验批次 (4 GPU 利用状态 + 3 个新实验)

**状态快照** (2026-05-25 00:05 UTC):
- GPU 0 (100%, 74GB): `random_subset_epoch_seed42` — step 3904/6103 (64%), 正常推进
- GPU 1 (100%, 46GB): `p0_nmin_5` — step 57/6103 (<1%), 刚启动
- GPU 2 (99%, 46GB): `detect_remove_q05_seed42` — step ~120/6103 (2%), 近期重启
- GPU 3 (100%, 46GB): `permuted_score_control_seed42` — step 69/6103 (~1%), 刚启动

全 4 GPU 满负荷，资源利用率良好。

---

### 发现

**1. [severity: warning] `permuted_score_control_seed42` 使用 τ=0.8，与主实验 τ=0.7 不匹配**

spec 显示 `--tau 0.8`，但主实验（adaptive_fix_seed42 等所有 N=5 seeds）均使用 `--tau 0.7`。Step metrics 确认 tau_batch=0.8 从 step 0 开始。

作为 score 排列控制实验，应与 treatment 组使用完全相同的超参数（除 score 排列外），否则引入混淆变量。τ=0.8 触发更多 batch 进入 reweighting（α_eff < 0.8 的 batch 更多），对比不公。

**建议**：实验仍在 step 69/6103，损失极小。建议 kill 后以 `--tau 0.7` 重启。

**2. [severity: warning] `p0_batchsize_16` 注册表状态陈旧**

注册表显示 `p0_batchsize_16` 为 RUNNING，但 Worker 已报告 OOM（18.55 GiB logits tensor 超出 GPU 剩余显存）。GPU 1 上实际运行的是 `p0_nmin_5`（PID 963054，cmdline 确认 `--output_dir .../p0_nmin_5 --n_min 5`）。同时 `p0_nmin_5` 注册表状态仍为 PREPARING。

需更新：p0_batchsize_16 → FAILED (OOM)，p0_nmin_5 → RUNNING (pid=963054)。

**3. [severity: info] `p0_nmin_5` 功能上与 nmin4_seed42 重复**

n_min=5 > B=4 → ESS ≥ 5 对 batch_size=4 不可能满足 → b*=0 始终成立。Step metrics 确认 b=0.0, ess=4.0。nmin4_seed42 已展示完全相同行为（b*=0 100%, PPL=17.6947 ≈ uniform 17.694）。

作为 n_min 灵敏度曲线的边界点可接受，但不会产生新信息。如果 GPU 时间紧张可考虑让出。

**4. [severity: info] `detect_remove_q05_seed42` 近期重启**

注册表 PID=601609（2026-05-21 创建），实际 PID=962199。当前 step ~120/6103，~2.08s/it，已运行约 4 分钟。说明近期（可能在服务器重启后）刚重新启动。训练正常进行中。

数据 153725 docs（41.4% 保留），用 uniform 权重训练 full 6103 steps。与之前 `dr_epoch_matched_indep_lr_seed42`（epoch-matched, PPL=18.78）设计不同——这次给 filtered 数据 full token budget（multi-epoch），更公平的 D&R hard-filtering baseline。

**5. [severity: info] 新实验均未在 experiment_plan.yaml 中注册**

`permuted_score_control_seed42` 和 `detect_remove_q05_seed42` 没有对应的 experiment_plan entry（后者映射的是旧的 dr_epoch_matched 实验）。建议在 plan 中补充 entry 以便覆盖率追踪。

---

**结论: WARNING**（τ=0.8 confound + 注册表状态陈旧）

**优先行动建议**：
1. **P0**: Kill `permuted_score_control_seed42`，改 `--tau 0.7` 重启（仅损失 ~4 分钟训练）
2. **P0**: 更新注册表 — p0_batchsize_16 → FAILED, p0_nmin_5 → RUNNING

[Reviewer 回复]:
## 审查报告 — CRITICAL: 磁盘满 + 连锁实验崩溃

**[severity: critical] /root/autodl-tmp 磁盘 100% 已满 (250G/250G, 仅剩 200K)**

### 已发生的事故

1. **p0_nmin_5** 在 step 500（首次 checkpoint save）崩溃：
   ```
   RuntimeError: [enforce fail at inline_container.cc:668] . unexpected pos 86016 vs 85908
   ```
   torch.save optimizer state 失败。GPU 1 现已空闲 (0%, 0 MiB)。

2. **detect_remove_q05_seed42** 旧进程 (PID 601613, May 21 启动) 在 step 5827/6103 (95.5%) 被 SIGKILL（EXIT_CODE=137）。cron on_failed 触发的是这个旧进程。**新进程 (PID 962199) 仍在 GPU 2 上运行，step ~499。**

### 即将发生的崩溃（紧急）

所有 3 个存活实验将在下一个 checkpoint save 崩溃：

| 实验 | GPU | 当前 step | 下次 save | 距崩溃 |
|------|-----|-----------|-----------|--------|
| random_subset_epoch_seed42 | 0 | ~3904/6103 | step 4000 | **~3 分钟** |
| detect_remove_q05_seed42 (new) | 2 | ~499/6103 | step 500 | **即将** |
| permuted_score_control_seed42 | 3 | ~500+/6103 | step 500/1000 | **即将/已崩** |

### 根因

models/ 目录占 **227G**。大量 DONE 实验保留了 2 个完整 checkpoint（各 3.4G = 6.8G/实验）+ final（1.2G）= 7.9G/实验。已评估完毕的实验不需要保留 checkpoint。

粗估可回收空间：~20 个 DONE 实验 × 6.7G checkpoint = **~130G**。

### 紧急行动建议（按优先级）

1. **立即**: 删除已评估 DONE 实验的 checkpoint-* 目录（保留 final/），释放 ~130G。需要 Worker 用 ssh_execute 执行。以下实验安全删除 checkpoint：
   - uniform_seed789, uniform_seed234, adaptive_seed789, adaptive_seed234
   - bs8_nmin4_* (6 个), rho03_* 系列, aeff_only_* (3 个)
   - 以及其他 DONE 且已评估的实验

2. **同时**: 检查 random_subset_epoch_seed42 是否已在 step 4000 崩溃。如果还活着，磁盘清理完后它可以继续。

3. **之后**: 重启 p0_nmin_5（如果仍需要——此实验功能上与 nmin4_seed42 重复）。

4. **之后**: 检查 detect_remove 和 permuted_score 是否存活或需要重启。

**注意**: detect_remove_q05_seed42 的旧 checkpoint（5500/5000/4500, May 21）来自旧进程。如果旧进程的训练是有效的，可以从 checkpoint-5500 resume 而非从头开始，节省 ~3 小时。但需确认旧进程的命令与新进程是否兼容。

[Reviewer 回复]:
## 审查报告 — p0_nmin_5

**结论**: WARNING

### 发现

- [severity: warning] **实验设计退化：n_min=5 > B=4 导致 adaptive weighting 数学上等价于 uniform。** 日志中从 step 0 到 step 500（crash 前全部记录），`weighting/b` **恒等于 0**，`weighting/ess` **恒等于 4**（= B），`unweighted_loss == weighted_loss` 全程成立。原因：ESS 约束要求 ESS ≥ n_min=5，但 batch size B=4 时 ESS 的最大值就是 B=4（当所有权重相等时取到），5 > 4 永远不可能满足，b* 被强制为 0。**这不是"更频繁地退化为 uniform"，而是 100% 保证退化为 uniform。** 即使重跑成功，结果也会与已有的 uniform baseline（如 uniform_seed42, holdout_ppl=17.694）在噪声范围内一致，**不提供新信息**。如果要测试 n_min=5 的 sensitivity，必须使用 B ≥ n_min（例如 B=8 配 n_min=5）。

- [severity: info] **Crash 根因是磁盘 I/O 错误，非"进程多次死亡"。** 日志明确显示 step 500 保存 checkpoint 时 `RuntimeError: basic_ios::clear: iostream error` → `RuntimeError: [enforce fail at inline_container.cc:668] unexpected pos 86016 vs 85908`（torch 写 optimizer.pt 失败）。Registry 记录的 failure_reason "process dead 5× with no sentinel/W&B — auto-resolved as failed" 是 auto-monitor 的误判，真实原因是磁盘写入异常。当前 `/root/autodl-tmp` 使用率 90%（224G/250G）。同时间 `permuted_score_control_seed42` 也在同一服务器 crash，可能是同一磁盘问题。

- [severity: info] **GPU 合规**：cuda:1 在项目分配范围（cuda:0-3）内，无越界。

### 建议

1. **不要重跑 p0_nmin_5**——即使成功也是 uniform 的冗余复制。如果 n_min sensitivity curve 需要 n_min=5 这个数据点，应在 B=8 配置下测试（B=8, n_min=5），或将当前 B=4 实验的 n_min=4 结论直接标注为"n_min ≥ B 时退化为 uniform"的理论边界。
2. 清理 `/root/autodl-tmp` 上的旧 checkpoint（已 90% 满），防止后续实验再次 crash。
3. 修正 registry 中 p0_nmin_5 的 failure_reason 为真实原因（disk I/O error at step 500）。

## 审查报告 — permuted_score_control_seed42

**结论**: WARNING

### 发现

- [severity: warning] **Crash 根因：磁盘满。** 日志明确显示 step 500 保存 checkpoint 时 `OSError: [Errno 28] No space left on device: '/root/autodl-tmp/provenance_weight_training/output/models/permuted_score_control_seed42/checkpoint-500'`。与同时间 crash 的 p0_nmin_5 为同一根因。Registry 记录的 "process dead 5× with no sentinel/W&B" 是 auto-monitor 误判，真实原因是 `/root/autodl-tmp` 磁盘空间耗尽。当前磁盘已恢复至 90%（27G 可用），但仍偏高。

- [severity: info] **Adaptive weighting 在 permuted scores 上正常工作。** 与 p0_nmin_5 不同，本实验日志显示 `weighting/b` 非零且波动正常（0, 0.6, 0.72, 1.318 等），`ess` 在 2.0-4.0 间变化，`weighted_loss ≠ unweighted_loss`。说明 permuted scores 仍有足够的分数方差让 adaptive weighting 产生非平凡权重。这是好事——如果 permuted 实验跑完后 PPL 劣于 uniform，就能证明 provenance signal 的 load-bearing 性。

- [severity: info] **GPU 合规**：cuda:3，在项目分配范围内。

### 建议

1. **值得重跑。** 本实验是 permuted score control，是证明 provenance signal 非 trivial 的关键对照实验，科学价值高。建议清理磁盘后优先安排重跑。
2. 重跑前先清理 `/root/autodl-tmp` 上的旧 checkpoint（当前 224G/250G），确保至少 30G+ 可用空间（单次实验 checkpoint 约 1.3G × save_total_limit=2 = ~2.6G，加 optimizer 约 3.5G）。
3. 可考虑加 `--save_only_model` 减少 checkpoint 体积（不保存 optimizer state），或降低 `--save_total_limit`。

1	---
2	description: 用 PaperBanana 生成高品质学术概念图（架构/流程/framework）。统计图不要用 PaperBanana，用 draw-paper-figure。需要 GOOGLE_API_KEY。
3	name: paper-banana
4	scope: global
5	tags:
6	  - visualization
7	  - paper
8	  - matplotlib
9	  - llm-codegen
10	  - conceptual-diagram
11	version: 4
12	tools:
13	  - request_secret
14	  - poll_secret_request
15	---
16	
17	# Paper Banana — 高品质学术概念图生成
18	
19	PaperBanana 由 Gemini Pro 自动写 matplotlib 代码，VLM Critic 评估渲染结果并迭代优化，输出顶会级别 300 DPI 概念图。
20	
21	**质量底线：每张图必须达到顶会论文的美观度——高级感、专业感、精致感。绝不接受 matplotlib 默认样式的廉价感。**
22	
23	## 适用范围
24	
25	| 场景 | 用什么 |
26	|------|--------|
27	| **概念图**（架构图、流程图、framework 示意） | **PaperBanana** ← 本 skill |
28	| **统计图**（scatter、bar、line、heatmap、forest） | **draw-paper-figure.skill.md**。**不要用 PaperBanana 画统计图** |
29	| 离线 / 无 GOOGLE_API_KEY | **draw-paper-figure.skill.md** |
30	
31	## 执行环境
32	
33	**PaperBanana 在本机（EC2）运行，不要 SSH 到远程服务器。**
34	
35	EC2（ap-northeast-1 东京）可直接访问 Gemini API，无需代理。
36	
37	## 前置条件
38	
39	### Step 1：获取 GOOGLE_API_KEY
40	
41	```python
42	request_secret(key_id="google_api_key", project="&lt;project_name>", purpose="PaperBanana 概念图生成")
43	# 等待 Director 审批后
44	poll_secret_request(request_id="<返回的 request_id>", project="&lt;project_name>")
45	```
46	
47	如果密钥尚未存储，告知 Director 需要 GOOGLE_API_KEY。
48	
49	### Step 2：安装验证（每次使用前必须检查）
50	
51	```bash
52	# 检查是否已安装
53	paperbanana --version 2>/dev/null || {
54	  echo "PaperBanana 未安装，正在安装..."
55	  pip install paperbanana -q
56	  paperbanana --version
57	}
58	```
59	
60	如果 `paperbanana --version` 失败（命令不存在），必须先 `pip install paperbanana -q` 安装。版本需 ≥ 0.1.2。
61	
62	**设计前必读** `skills/global/paper-visualization.skill.md` 的概念图 L1（防丑 7 条）+ L2（叙事 6 条）+ L3 Pattern + Checklist。统计图部分不适用于 PaperBanana。
63	
64	## 命令：generate
65	
66	概念图用 `generate` 子命令。**必须显式指定 `--vlm-model gemini-2.5-pro`**（默认模型 `gemini-2.0-flash` 已废弃，不指定会崩溃）。**`--iterations 5`**。
67	
68	## 完整流程
69	
70	### Step 1：写意图描述文件
71	
72	将详细设计描述写入文件（太长不适合命令行参数）：
73	
74	```bash
75	cat > /tmp/fig_intent.txt << 'INTENT'

### 发现

- [severity: info] **磁盘清理成功**：根分区 33%（21GB 空余），数据盘 56%（111GB 空余），已脱离 critical 状态。模型目录仍占 117GB，目录结构完整，safetensors 文件均保留。

- [severity: warning] **Registry 与实际进程不同步（3 个实验）**：以下实验在 registry 中标记为 DONE（failed/killed），但服务器上已被 Agent 重启并正在运行：
  1. `p0_nmin_5`：registry 显示 DONE/negative（16:47 May24），实际 PID 966103 于 00:48 May25 启动，当前 step ~50/6103（1%），运行在 cuda:1
  2. `permuted_score_control_seed42`：registry 显示 DONE/failed（16:44 May24），实际 PID 966241 于 00:48 May25 启动，当前 step ~50/6103（1%），运行在 cuda:3
  3. `detect_remove_q05_seed42`：registry 显示 DONE/killed（16:24 May24），实际 PID 962199 正在运行，当前 step 6555/8477（77%），运行在 cuda:2
  
  **这些实验的结果无法被正确追踪。** Agent 需要更新 registry 状态为 RUNNING 并记录新的 PID/启动时间，否则完成时的 metrics 将无处记录。

- [severity: info] **运行中实验状态健康**：
  - `random_subset_epoch_seed42`：step 3999/6103（65.5%），loss 正常下降（~2.0-2.3），cuda:0
  - 全部 4 张 GPU 利用率正常（35-100%），显存分配合理

- [severity: info] **GPU 分配未越界**：4 张 RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell 均在使用中，未发现越界进程。

### 建议
- Agent 应立即更新 p0_nmin_5、permuted_score_control_seed42、detect_remove_q05_seed42 的 registry 状态为 RUNNING，补充新 PID 和启动时间，确保实验完成后指标能正确记录。

## 审查报告 — worker_train_restart_nmin5_and_permuted_20260524T164714

**结论**: CRITICAL

### 发现

- [severity: critical] **p0_nmin_5 被 GPU 冲突杀死，registry 未更新**：
  - restart worker 于 00:48 启动 p0_nmin_5（PID 966103）在 cuda:1
  - cuda1_replacement worker 随后在同一 cuda:1 启动 contamination_20_tau07_seed42（PID 967103），导致 p0_nmin_5 在 step 80/6103（1.3%）被杀
  - **Registry 仍显示 p0_nmin_5 为 RUNNING**，但进程已不存在
  - 训练无 checkpoint 保存（仅 config.json + step_metrics.jsonl + train.log）
  - **根因**：两个 worker 对 cuda:1 的分配冲突——restart worker 占用了 cuda:1 给 p0_nmin_5，replacement worker 又把 cuda:1 分给 contamination_20_tau07_seed42，没有检查 GPU 是否被占用

- [severity: info] **permuted_score_control_seed42 正常运行**：PID 966241 在 cuda:3，elapsed 5:38 min，训练正常推进。

- [severity: info] **当前 GPU 实际分配**（nvidia-smi 核实）：
  - cuda:0 — PID 944565（random_subset_epoch_seed42），74GB VRAM
  - cuda:1 — PID 967103（contamination_20_tau07_seed42），46GB VRAM，step 62/6103
  - cuda:2 — PID 962199（detect_remove_q05_seed42），74GB VRAM，~85% 进度
  - cuda:3 — PID 966241（permuted_score_control_seed42），46GB VRAM

- [severity: warning] **detect_remove_q05_seed42 registry 仍未修复**（第三次提醒）。

### 建议
1. **立即更新 p0_nmin_5 registry 为 failed**（被 GPU 冲突杀死）
2. **p0_nmin_5 需要重新安排**——等 cuda:1 上 contamination_20_tau07_seed42 完成后（~3.5h），或 cuda:2 上 detect_remove_q05_seed42 完成后（~5min）立即提交
3. **Worker 协调机制需修复**：同一 GPU 被两个 worker 同时分配导致实验丢失，建议 Director 在分配 GPU 前检查当前占用

- 第 4 轮：修复 connector arrow 渲染（FancyArrowPatch -|>），加宽 annotation box
- 第 5 轮：增大 annotation 字号到 7pt+，最终 polish

## 修改内容

1. **生成文件**：`artifacts/figures/fig1_overview.{png,pdf}`（300 DPI，7.0×2.15in）
2. **introduction.tex**：删除 TikZ 代码块（27 行），改为：

   ```
3. **生成脚本**：`/tmp/gen_fig1.py`（可复现）

## 图片内容

- 5 阶段水平 pipeline：Mixed corpus → Detector → Reweight → Train → Evaluate
- 圆角矩形 + 语义配色（蓝=Detector, 橙=Reweight, 绿=Train, 灰=端点）

- 带圆角边框的 annotation box + 彩色 connector arrow

## 编译状态

- 15 页，0 critical warnings，PASS
- Figure 1 位于 page 3 顶部（`figure*` 全宽浮动特性）
- 主文 §1-§7 仍在 page 8 内

_source line 1230 · 2026-05-24T17:10:28.074565+00:00_

1	The detect-and-reweight paradigm~\citep{drayson2025detection} has emerged as a response to synthetic contamination in web corpora, now estimated at 20--60\% of crawled data~\citep{cheng2025contamination}.
2	Training on such data risks model collapse~\citep{shumailov2024model,dohmatob2025strong}, motivating methods that downweight likely-synthetic samples via $w(x) = (1 - q(x))^b$, where $q(x)$ is a detector's synthetic probability and $b$ is a sharpening exponent.
3	An alternative, Detect and Remove (D\&R), hard-filters samples above a threshold~\citep{feng2025beyond}.
4	
5	Despite growing adoption, the paradigm's effectiveness conditions are poorly characterized.
6	Does D\&R genuinely outperform soft reweighting, or do published comparisons confound filtering quality with multi-epoch exposure?
7	What detector properties are necessary for effective reweighting?
8	How does the reweighting mechanism behave across contamination levels, and can $b$ be set without per-model grid search?
9	
10	We investigate these questions through controlled experiments on Qwen3-0.6B continued pretraining (132M FineWeb tokens, $\rho{=}0.4$, 5 seeds), with extensions to $\rho \in \{0.2, 0.6\}$ and 1.7B parameters (\S\ref{sec:limitations} discusses scope).
11	To ground the analysis of exponent selection, we implement a dual-constrained formulation as an illustrative application: find the minimum $b$ such that $\aeff(b) \geq \tau$ (quality floor, motivated by convergence theory~\citep{wang2026survive}), subject to $\mathrm{ESS}(b) \geq \nmin$ (stability ceiling)~\citep{kish1965survey,vehtari2024psis}.
12	
13	Our analysis yields three findings:
14	\begin{itemize}[nosep,leftmargin=*]
15	\item \textbf{Epoch confound in D\&R evaluation.}
16	The bulk of D\&R's PPL advantage over adaptive reweighting (${>}$95\%) is attributable to multi-epoch exposure, not filtering quality; epoch-matched controls ($N{=}3$) reduce the gap from 0.396 to $\Delta{=}0.017$ (Table~\ref{tab:epoch_match}).
17	
18	\ite

{
  "text": "Figure 1 已用 PaperBanana 迭代了 7 个版本，最好的两个版本：\n- **v3**（无图标、直线箭头、subtle 填充 + 圆角标注框）\n- **v4**（白底 + 彩色左边框、极简文字标注）\n\n但 PaperBanana 底层使用 Gemini Imagen（AI 图像生成），对精确几何管线图有天然限制：\n1. Box 大小无法精确相等\n2. 填充色无法做到真正 subtle（模型默认 30-40% 饱和度）\n3. 文字是 AI 渲染的（非矢量，放大后不够 crisp）\n4. 边框粗细无法精确控制\n\n**建议**：改用 matplotlib 手写代码（FancyBboxPatch + FancyArrowPatch），可以做到像素级精确控制——等大 box、精确色值、矢量文字、一致线宽。这种管线图其实更适合用代码精确绘制。\n\n是否允许回退 matplotlib？"
}
```

</details>

1	\subsection{Main Results}
2	\label{sec:main-results}
3	
4	Table~\ref{tab:main} reports held-out perplexity at $\rho = 0.4$ across five seeds.
5	Continued pretraining reduces PPL from 22.970 to the 17.3--17.8 range; method comparisons operate within a narrow residual.
6	
7	Adaptive $\bstar$ achieves 5-seed mean PPL 17.674, compared to 17.697 for uniform ($\Delta{=}{-}0.023$, directionally consistent across all seeds) and 17.620 for grid $b{=}0.3$ (3-seed mean, post-hoc oracle; gap $+$0.054; Table~\ref{tab:aux}).
8	A paired $t$-test yields $t(4){=}{-}8.21$, $p{=}0.0012$; after Bonferroni--Holm correction for nine family-wise comparisons, $p_{\mathrm{BH}}{=}0.011$, reaching conventional significance at $\alpha{=}0.05$.
9	The engineering benefit is eliminating $b$ grid search (nine full training runs~\citep{drayson2025detection}, ${\sim}$16 GPU-hours at 0.6B scale, scaling linearly with model size), though grid search yields a larger improvement.
10	The grid-best $b{=}0.3$ is a post-hoc oracle from nine candidates with $5.5{\times}$ higher cross-seed standard deviation than adaptive ($\sigma{=}0.033$ vs.\ $0.006$); the noisy PPL plateau at $b \in [0.1, 0.5]$ (Figure~\ref{fig:grid_b_trend}) makes validation-based selection unreliable.
11	At the nearest fixed point ($b{=}1.0$), PPL equals uniform (17.692 vs.\ 17.694): per-batch adaptation is essential.
12	
13	The ablation hierarchy at $\rho{=}0.4$ (Adaptive ${<}$ Uniform ${<}$ ESS-only ${<}$ Random, Table~\ref{tab:main}) reveals two requirements for effective reweighting: detector signal and minimum intervention.
14	Random weights $\mathcal{U}(0,2)$ (17.802) confirm that detector $q$-scores carry genuine provenance signal; uninformed reweighting only increases gradient variance.
15	ESS-only (mean $17.737 {\pm} 0.021$) uses the same $q$-scores but maximizes $b$ up to the stability ceiling, concentrating weights on a few high-confidence samples; it degrades PPL by 0.043 relative to uniform.

{
  "description": "Draw Figure A: weight mechanism",
  "prompt": "Write a publication-quality matplotlib figure and save it to `/tmp/fig1_candidate_A.png` (300 DPI, PNG). This is a potential \"Figure 1\" for an EMNLP paper about detect-and-reweight methods for synthetic contamination in LLM pretraining.\n\n**Concept**: Show how the reweighting function w(x) = (1−q(x))^b transforms detector scores into training weights, and how different b values produce very different weight distributions.\n\n**Layout**: Two panels side by side, figsize (10, 3.5), white background.\n\n**Panel (a) \"Weighting function\"** (left, ~55% width):\n- Plot w = (1−q)^b for q ∈ [0, 1] for three b values:\n  - b = 0.3 (adaptive b*, label \"Adaptive b* ≈ 0.6\")  \n  - b = 1.0 (fixed, label \"Fixed b = 1.0\")\n  - b = 3.5 (ESS-only, label \"ESS-only b ≈ 3.5\")\n- Use colors: #4A90D9 (blue) for adaptive, #7F8C8D (gray) for fixed, #E67E22 (orange) for ESS-only\n- X-axis: \"Detector score q(x)\" — 0 to 1\n- Y-axis: \"Sample weight w(x)\" — 0 to 1\n- Add a vertical dashed line at q = 0.5 (threshold) in light gray\n- Add text annotation \"likely synthetic →\" near q=0.8 area, small, gray italic\n- Add text annotation \"← likely human\" near q=0.2 area, small, gray italic\n- Serif font for axis labels, sans-serif for annotations\n- Thin spines (left + bottom only), no grid\n\n**Panel (b) \"Effect on batch weights\"** (right, ~45% width):\n- Show a simulated batch of 16 samples as horizontal bar chart or lollipop plot\n- Generate 16 q-scores from a mixture: ~60% from Beta(2,3) (more human) and ~40% from Beta(3,1.5) (more synthetic) — this simulates the detector's q-score distribution\n- For each sample, show the weight under adaptive (b=0.6) vs ESS-only (b=3.5)\n- Sort samples by q-score\n- Use two sets of dots/bars side by side, colored blue (adaptive) and orange (ESS-only)\n- X-axis: \"Weight w(x)\"\n- Y-axis: \"Samples (sorted by q)\"\n- Add a thin vertical dashed line at w=1 (uniform weight)\n- Key insight: ESS-only crushes most weights near 0, adaptive is gentler\n\n**Style**:\n- Panel labels \"(a)\" and \"(b)\" in 11pt bold, top-left of each panel\n- Muted academic palette: blue #4A90D9, orange #E67E22, gray #7F8C8D\n- NO matplotlib defaults: no gray background, no thick gridlines, no top/right spines\n- Line width 1.5 for curves, 0.8 for axes\n- Font sizes: axis labels 10pt, tick labels 8pt, legend 8pt\n- Tight layout with wspace=0.35\n- Use `plt.rcParams['font.family'] = 'serif'` for main text\n- Set random seed 42 for reproducibility\n\nRun the script to generate the image, then verify it exists.\n",

\"D&R (epoch-matched)\" — PPL = 17.657, bar color #C0392B (lighter/desaturated red), label shows \"~1.0 epoch\"\n  - \"Adaptive b*\" — PPL = 17.674, bar color #4A90D9 (blue), label shows \"1.0 epoch\"\n  - {
  "description": "Draw Figure C: dual constraint",
  "prompt": "Write a publication-quality matplotlib figure and save it to `/tmp/fig1_candidate_C.png` (300 DPI, PNG). This is a potential \"Figure 1\" for an EMNLP paper.\n\n**Concept**: Show how the dual-constraint mechanism selects the optimal sharpening exponent b*. The paper's method finds the MINIMUM b such that α_eff(b) ≥ τ (quality floor) AND ESS(b) ≥ n_min (stability ceiling). The key insight is \"minimum intervention\" — don't sharpen more than necessary.\n\n**Layout**: Two panels, figsize (10, 3.5), white background.\n\n**Panel (a) \"Dual constraint on a single batch\"** (left, ~55% width):\n- X-axis: \"Sharpening exponent b\" from 0 to 5\n- Y-axis (left): \"α_eff(b)\" from 0 to 1 — plot as blue curve (#4A90D9)\n- Y-axis (right): \"ESS(b)\" from 0 to 4 — plot as teal curve (#3D9B7A)\n\nSimulate curves for a representative batch:\n- α_eff(b): starts at ~0.5 when b=0, increases monotonically, approaching 1.0. Use: α_eff = 1 - 0.5*exp(-1.2*b)\n- ESS(b): starts at 4 (= batch size) when b=0, decreases monotonically. Use: ESS = 4 * exp(-0.4*b)\n\n- Draw horizontal dashed line at α_eff = 0.7 (τ threshold), blue, labeled \"τ = 0.7\"\n- Draw horizontal dashed line at ESS = 2 (n_min), teal, labeled \"n_min = 2\"\n- The α_eff constraint gives a LOWER bound on b (where α_eff first crosses τ)\n- The ESS constraint gives an UPPER bound on b (where ESS drops below n_min)\n- Shade the feasible region between these two bounds in light gray\n- Mark b* (the lower bound = minimum intervention) with a vertical red dashed line and red dot, labeled \"b*\"\n- Mark b_ESS (the upper bound) with a vertical orange dashed line, labeled \"b_ESS\"\n- Add text \"feasible region\" in the shaded area, small, gray\n\n**Panel (b) \"Minimum intervention vs. ESS-only\"** (right, ~45% width):\n- This panel shows the PPL curve from the grid search results, demonstrating why minimum b is better\n- X-axis: \"Fixed b\" from 0 to 5\n- Y-axis: \"Holdout PPL\" \n- Plot data points (from the paper's grid search, seed 42):\n  - b=0.1: PPL≈17.67, b=0.2: PPL≈17.65, b=0.3: PPL≈17.62, b=0.5: PPL≈17.66\n  - b=1.0: PPL≈17.69, b=1.5: PPL≈17.72, b=2.0: PPL≈17.75, b=3.0: PPL≈17.80, b=5.0: PPL≈17.95\n  (approximate values showing a U-shape with minimum around b=0.3)\n- Plot as scatter with connecting line, color #2C3E50\n- Draw horizontal dashed line for uniform baseline PPL = 17.694, gray, labeled \"uniform\"\n- Shade region b ≥ 2 in light red (degradation zone)\n- Mark \"Adaptive b* ≈ 0.9\" with blue vertical band/line\n- Mark \"ESS-only b ≈ 3.5\" with orange vertical band/line  \n- Add annotation arrow showing ESS-only is in degradation zone while adaptive is near optimum\n\n**Style**:\n- Panel labels \"(a)\" and \"(b)\" in 11pt bold, top-left\n- Muted academic palette: blue #4A90D9, teal #3D9B7A, orange #E67E22, dark #2C3E50\n- NO matplotlib defaults: no gray background, no gridlines, no top/right spines\n- Left + bottom spines only (and right spine for panel a's secondary y-axis), thin 0.8pt\n- Font: serif, 10pt axis labels, 8pt tick labels\n- Tight layout, wspace=0.35\n- `plt.rcParams['font.family'] = 'serif'`\n\nRun the script to generate the image, then verify it exists.\n",
  "run_in_background": "True"
}

[飞书用户 用户]: 📝 论文自检任务：缩写/专有名词定义检查

请对当前论文做一次缩写定义自检。AI 写作常见问题：很多专有名词的缩写（acronym）从第一次出现就没给出全称，整篇论文都默认读者认识。这不符合学术写作规范。

自检要求：

1. 通读全文，列出所有缩写词（包括方法名、数据集名、指标名、模型名）
2. 对每个缩写，确认它在【第一次出现时】用了 "全称 (缩写)" 格式
   ✅ 正确：Large Language Model (LLM) ... 后续可直接用 LLM
   ❌ 错误：摘要里用 LLM，但全文从未给出 Large Language Model
3. 特别关注以下高发区：
   - 你自己造的方法名/算法名（例如 SCALE、TRACE、PIVOT 这类）——是首字母缩写还是单纯命名？如果是缩写，必须说明展开
   - 数据集/benchmark 名（MMLU、ARC、GSM8K 等常见 benchmark 也要在首次出现时给全称）
   - 指标缩写（F1、AUC、BLEU、ROUGE-L 等）
   - 领域术语（RLHF、DPO、CoT、ICL、PEFT 等）
4. Abstract 和 Introduction 是首次出现的高发区，Abstract 里的缩写也要在 Abstract 里展开一次

修完后请简短汇报修了哪些。

### Tool result

_source line 1398 · 2026-05-24T18:24:47.935980+00:00_

1	As synthetic text pervades web corpora, detect-and-reweight methods---downweighting likely-synthetic samples during training---have emerged to prevent model collapse, yet when they actually help remains unclear.
2	We investigate this question through controlled continued-pretraining experiments (Qwen3-0.6B, 132M tokens, 5 seeds) and identify three effectiveness conditions.
3	First, published detect-and-remove evaluations are confounded by multi-epoch exposure: over 95\% of the reported advantage vanishes under epoch-matched controls.
4	Second, the detector must provide both sufficient discrimination \emph{and} good calibration; high-AUC detectors with poor calibration degrade performance below uniform training.
5	Third, minimum intervention---applying the smallest reweighting exponent that meets a quality floor---outperforms unconstrained weight maximization.
6	As an illustrative application of the minimum-intervention principle, we derive a dual-constrained adaptive exponent $\bstar$ that improves over uniform training at contamination ratios $\rho \geq 0.4$, reaching conventional significance after multiple-comparison correction, while requiring no per-model tuning.
7	These findings apply within our self-contamination setup at 0.6B scale; generalization to cross-model contamination and larger models remains to be established.
8

_source line 1400 · 2026-05-24T18:24:48.338550+00:00_

1	The detect-and-reweight paradigm~\citep{drayson2025detection} has emerged as a response to synthetic contamination in web corpora, now estimated at 20--60\% of crawled data~\citep{cheng2025contamination}.
2	Training on such data risks model collapse~\citep{shumailov2024model,dohmatob2025strong}, motivating methods that downweight likely-synthetic samples via $w(x) = (1 - q(x))^b$, where $q(x)$ is a detector's synthetic probability and $b$ is a sharpening exponent.
3	An alternative, Detect and Remove (D\&R), hard-filters samples above a threshold~\citep{feng2025beyond}.
4	
5	Despite growing adoption, the paradigm's effectiveness conditions are poorly characterized.
6	Does D\&R genuinely outperform soft reweighting, or do published comparisons confound filtering quality with multi-epoch exposure?
7	What detector properties are necessary for effective reweighting?
8	How does the reweighting mechanism behave across contamination levels, and can $b$ be set without per-model grid search?
9	
10	We investigate these questions through controlled experiments on Qwen3-0.6B continued pretraining (132M FineWeb tokens, $\rho{=}0.4$, 5 seeds), with extensions to $\rho \in \{0.2, 0.6\}$ and 1.7B parameters (\S\ref{sec:limitations} discusses scope).
11	To ground the analysis of exponent selection, we implement a dual-constrained formulation as an illustrative application: find the minimum $b$ such that $\aeff(b) \geq \tau$ (quality floor, motivated by convergence theory~\citep{wang2026survive}), subject to $\mathrm{ESS}(b) \geq \nmin$ (stability ceiling)~\citep{kish1965survey,vehtari2024psis}.
12	
13	Our analysis yields three findings:
14	\begin{itemize}[nosep,leftmargin=*]
15	\item \textbf{Epoch confound in D\&R evaluation.}
16	The bulk of D\&R's PPL advantage over adaptive reweighting (${>}$95\%) is attributable to multi-epoch exposure, not filtering quality; epoch-matched controls ($N{=}3$) reduce the gap from 0.396 to $\Delta{=}0.017$ (Table~\ref{tab:epoch_match}).
17	
18	\ite

_source line 1402 · 2026-05-24T18:24:48.391081+00:00_

1	\paragraph{Synthetic Contamination and Model Collapse.}
2	Training on recursively generated data leads to irreversible quality degradation (\emph{model collapse})~\citep{shumailov2024model}.
3	\citet{alemohammad2024mad} formalize this as Model Autophagy Disorder, showing that quality inevitably degrades without fresh real data.
4	More critically, \citet{dohmatob2025strong} prove under scaling-law regimes that even a vanishingly small synthetic fraction suffices to trigger collapse, ruling out data scaling as a remedy.
5	\citet{cheng2025contamination} confirm that synthetic content now constitutes an estimated 20--60\% of web corpora.
6	Not all synthetic data is harmful: the Phi series~\citep{gunasekar2023textbooks,li2023textbooks2} demonstrates that curated synthetic data can improve training efficiency, but this assumes intentional generation with quality control, unlike the uncontrolled web contamination we address.
7	
8	\paragraph{Detection, Filtering, and Data Reweighting.}
9	Multiple detectors produce continuous scores suitable for downstream weighting: DetectGPT~\citep{mitchell2023detectgpt} uses probability curvature, Binoculars~\citep{hans2024binoculars} cross-perplexity ratios, and Min-$k$\% Prob~\citep{shi2024minkprob} low-probability token statistics; \citet{wu2025survey} survey the broader detection landscape.
10	A parallel line of work optimizes data composition at the domain~\citep{xie2023doremi,xie2023dsir,ye2025mixing} or instance level~\citep{abbas2023semdedup,bengio2009curriculum,mindermann2022prioritized,sow2025dynamic}, using loss, frequency, quality, or domain membership as the selection signal; none targets synthetic provenance.
11	
12	Most directly related to our work, \citet{drayson2025detection} propose $w_j = (1{-}q_j)^b$ for collapse prevention via detect-and-reweight in a from-scratch pretraining setting, where $q_j$ is the detector's synthetic probability.
13	We adopt the same weighting formula but differ in several fundamental ways.
1

1	Let $\mathcal{D} = \mathcal{D}_{\mathrm{real}} \cup \mathcal{D}_{\mathrm{synth}}$ denote a pretraining corpus with token-level contamination ratio $\rho = |\mathcal{D}_{\mathrm{synth}}| / |\mathcal{D}|$, assumed unknown~\citep{cheng2025contamination}.
2	A detector assigns each sample $x$ a synthetic probability $q(x) \in [0,1]$; following \citet{drayson2025detection}, we weight each sample as $w(x) = (1 - q(x))^{b}$, where $b \geq 0$ is a sharpening exponent ($b{=}0$ recovers uniform training).
3	Rather than selecting $b$ via grid search~\citep{drayson2025detection}, we derive $b$ per-batch from the batch's $q$-score distribution using two opposing constraints.
4	
5	Given a batch $\mathcal{B} = \{x_1, \ldots, x_B\}$ with scores $q_i = q(x_i)$ and weights $w_i(b) = (1 - q_i)^{b}$:
6	
7	\paragraph{Quality floor.}
8	The \emph{effective authenticity} $\aeff(b) = \sum_i w_i(b)(1{-}q_i) / \sum_i w_i(b)$ measures the weighted mean detector confidence that samples are real.
9	We require $\aeff(b) \geq \tau$, yielding a lower bound $b_\tau$ on the exponent, motivated by convergence theory under contamination~\citep{wang2026survive}.
10	$\aeff$ correlates with the true batch-level real fraction at Pearson $r \approx 0.79$ (Appendix~\ref{app:alpha_validation}); limitations are discussed in \S\ref{sec:discussion}.
11	
12	\paragraph{Stability ceiling.}
13	Kish's effective sample size $\mathrm{ESS}(b) = (\sum_i w_i)^2 / \sum_i w_i^2$~\citep{kish1965survey} decreases monotonically in $b$ (Appendix~\ref{app:proofs}).
14	We require $\mathrm{ESS}(b) \geq \nmin$, yielding an upper bound $\bess$.
15	
16	\paragraph{Adaptive $\bstar$.}
17	Since $\aeff$ is non-decreasing and ESS is decreasing in $b$, the two constraints define an interval $[b_\tau, \bess]$.
18	The optimal exponent implements \emph{minimum intervention}:
19	\begin{equation}
20	  b^{*} = \begin{cases}
21	    0        & \text{if } \aeff(0) \geq \tau, \\
22	    b_\tau   & \text{if } b_\tau \leq \bess, \\
23	    \bess & \tex

1	\paragraph{Training Data.}
2	We sample a 132M-token subset of FineWeb~\citep{penedo2024fineweb} as the real-data source, comparable to typical domain-adaptive continued pretraining budgets~\citep{gururangan2020dont}.
3	To simulate web-scale synthetic contamination, we generate continuations with Qwen3-0.6B~\citep{yang2025qwen3}, the same architecture used for continued pretraining, and inject them at token-level ratio $\rho = 0.4$ via uniform random mixing.\footnote{$\rho$ denotes the token-level contamination ratio. Because synthetic documents are shorter on average (304 vs.\ 560 words), the document-level synthetic fraction is 55.6\% at $\rho{=}0.4$.}
4	This self-contamination design means generator and trainee share the same distribution, providing a controlled testbed with known ground-truth labels but limiting ecological validity; cross-model transfer results (Appendix~\ref{app:cross_model}) are mixed, and we discuss implications for detection difficulty in \S\ref{sec:limitations}.
5	
6	\paragraph{Detector.}
7	We train a TF-IDF + Logistic Regression classifier on a held-out labeled split (AUC\,=\,0.94, calibrated ECE\,=\,0.05 after temperature scaling with $T{=}0.204$).
8	The resulting $q$-scores have mean 0.60, standard deviation 0.37, and median 0.71, with 46.3\% of samples in the uncertain range $[0.2, 0.8]$.
9	We use this lightweight detector rather than neural alternatives (e.g., ModernBERT at AUC\,${\geq}$\,0.98 in \citealt{drayson2025detection}) to test whether score \emph{calibration} may matter as much as raw discrimination for reweighting.
10	To evaluate detector sensitivity, we additionally test two generative baselines: a GPT-2-based perplexity scorer (AUC\,=\,0.91) and an N-gram language model perplexity scorer (AUC\,=\,0.73); results are reported in \S\ref{sec:robustness} (implementation details in Appendix~\ref{app:detector_details}).
11	
12	\paragraph{Training.}
13	Table~\ref{tab:config} summarizes the training configuration.

1	\subsection{Main Results}
2	\label{sec:main-results}
3	
4	Table~\ref{tab:main} reports held-out perplexity at $\rho = 0.4$ across five seeds.
5	Continued pretraining reduces PPL from 22.970 to the 17.3--17.8 range; method comparisons operate within a narrow residual.
6	
7	Adaptive $\bstar$ achieves 5-seed mean PPL 17.674, compared to 17.697 for uniform ($\Delta{=}{-}0.023$, directionally consistent across all seeds) and 17.620 for grid $b{=}0.3$ (3-seed mean, post-hoc oracle; gap $+$0.054; Table~\ref{tab:aux}).
8	A paired $t$-test yields $t(4){=}{-}8.21$, $p{=}0.0012$; after Bonferroni--Holm correction for nine family-wise comparisons, $p_{\mathrm{BH}}{=}0.011$, reaching conventional significance at $\alpha{=}0.05$.
9	The engineering benefit is eliminating $b$ grid search (nine full training runs~\citep{drayson2025detection}, ${\sim}$16 GPU-hours at 0.6B scale, scaling linearly with model size), though grid search yields a larger improvement.
10	The grid-best $b{=}0.3$ is a post-hoc oracle from nine candidates with $5.5{\times}$ higher cross-seed standard deviation than adaptive ($\sigma{=}0.033$ vs.\ $0.006$); the noisy PPL plateau at $b \in [0.1, 0.5]$ (Figure~\ref{fig:grid_b_trend}) makes validation-based selection unreliable.
11	At the nearest fixed point ($b{=}1.0$), PPL equals uniform (17.692 vs.\ 17.694): per-batch adaptation is essential.
12	
13	The ablation hierarchy at $\rho{=}0.4$ (Adaptive ${<}$ Uniform ${<}$ ESS-only ${<}$ Random, Table~\ref{tab:main}) reveals two requirements for effective reweighting: detector signal and minimum intervention.
14	Random weights $\mathcal{U}(0,2)$ (17.802) confirm that detector $q$-scores carry genuine provenance signal; uninformed reweighting only increases gradient variance.
15	ESS-only (mean $17.737 {\pm} 0.021$) uses the same $q$-scores but maximizes $b$ up to the stability ceiling, concentrating weights on a few high-confidence samples; it degrades PPL by 0.043 relative to uniform.

1	\paragraph{The epoch confound in D\&R evaluation.}
2	The epoch confound identified in \S\ref{sec:main-results} accounts for ${>}$95\% of D\&R's reported advantage; the residual ($\Delta{=}0.017$, 3 seeds) falls within cross-seed variance and is robust to LR schedule choice ($\Delta{=}0.006$ under independent schedules).
3	This confound was invisible in \citet{drayson2025detection} because from-scratch pretraining on large corpora rarely completes even one epoch; in continued pretraining, aggressive D\&R filtering (retaining 31.5\% at $q{>}0.3$) creates ${\sim}$3.2 content epochs, making the confound quantitatively significant.
4	A permuted-score control ($+$0.26 vs.\ uniform) confirms that detector signal is load-bearing.
5	
6	\paragraph{When uniform beats reweighting.}
7	Table~\ref{tab:decision} summarizes three conditions, each necessary for effective reweighting: (i)~sufficient detector discrimination \emph{and} calibration (AUC\,$\geq$\,0.9, ECE\,$\leq$\,0.05; \S\ref{sec:robustness}); (ii)~contamination $\rho \geq 0.4$, below which the method degrades ($+$0.046 at $\rho{=}0.2$) with a sharp transition in $[0.3, 0.4]$ (Figure~\ref{fig:delta_vs_rho}, Appendix~\ref{app:rho03}); (iii)~generator--trainee mismatch (at 1.7B the benefit diminishes to $\Delta{=}{-}0.013$ with OOD mixed).
8	When any condition is violated, uniform is the safer default.

1	\begin{itemize}[nosep,leftmargin=*]
2	\item \textbf{Scope.}
3	All experiments use Qwen3 (0.6B and 1.7B) under self-contamination with 132--200M tokens of continued pretraining.
4	Generalization to other architectures, full pretraining scale ($>$100B tokens), or realistic multi-source web contamination---where generator--corpus distribution mismatch may reduce detector accuracy---remains untested.
5	
6	\item \textbf{Detector coverage.}
7	Only three detectors from two families (discriminative TF-IDF+LR and generative perplexity scorers) were evaluated; fine-tuned neural classifiers (e.g., ModernBERT) and zero-shot methods (e.g., DetectGPT, Binoculars) remain untested, so the AUC/ECE thresholds reported here may shift with broader detector coverage.
8	\end{itemize}
9

1	\section{Proofs}
2	\label{app:proofs}
3	
4	\begin{proposition}[$\alpha_{\mathrm{eff}}$ monotonicity]
5	\label{prop:monotone}
6	$\alpha_{\mathrm{eff}}(b)$ is non-decreasing in $b$, and strictly increasing whenever the batch contains at least two samples with distinct $q$-values.
7	\end{proposition}
8	
9	\begin{proof}[Proof of Proposition~\ref{prop:monotone}]
10	Let $w_i(b) = (1 - q_i)^b$, $W(b) = \sum_{j=1}^B w_j(b)$, and $\pi_i(b) = w_i(b) / W(b)$.
11	Then
12	\begin{equation*}
13	  \alpha_{\mathrm{eff}}(b) = \sum_{i=1}^{B} \pi_i(b)\, (1 - q_i).
14	\end{equation*}
15	Taking the derivative with respect to $b$:
16	\begin{align*}
17	  \frac{d\pi_i}{db}
18	  &= \pi_i \!\left(\frac{d \ln w_i}{db} - \frac{d \ln W}{db}\right) \\
19	  &= \pi_i \!\left(\ln(1{-}q_i) - \textstyle\sum_j \pi_j \ln(1{-}q_j)\right).
20	\end{align*}
21	Substituting,
22	\begin{align*}
23	  \frac{d\alpha_{\mathrm{eff}}}{db}
24	  &= \sum_i (1{-}q_i)\, \pi_i \!\left(\ln(1{-}q_i) - \mathbb{E}_{\pi}[\ln(1{-}q)]\right) \\
25	  &= \mathrm{Cov}_{\pi}\!\bigl(1{-}q_i,\, \ln(1{-}q_i)\bigr).
26	\end{align*}
27	Since $(1{-}q_i)$ and $\ln(1{-}q_i)$ are both increasing functions of $(1{-}q_i)$, they are positively associated, so the covariance is non-negative.
28	\end{proof}
29	
30	\begin{proposition}[ESS monotonicity]
31	\label{prop:ess_mono}
32	$\mathrm{ESS}(b)$ is non-increasing in $b$, and strictly decreasing whenever the batch contains at least two samples with distinct $q$-values.
33	\end{proposition}
34	
35	\begin{proof}
36	Write $\mathrm{ESS}(b) = 1 / \sum_i \pi_i(b)^2$, where $\pi_i(b) = w_i(b) / W(b)$.
37	The denominator $\sum_i \pi_i^2$ is the Herfindahl index $H$.
38	Differentiating $H$ with respect to $b$:
39	\begin{equation*}
40	  \frac{dH}{db} = 2 \sum_i \pi_i^2 \bigl(\ln(1{-}q_i) - \mathbb{E}_{\pi}[\ln(1{-}q)]\bigr).
41	\end{equation*}
42	Since $\pi_i \propto (1{-}q_i)^b$, larger $\pi_i$ implies larger $\ln(1{-}q_i)$; the $\pi^2$-weighted sum assigns greater mass to terms where $\ln(1{-}q_i) > \ma
…[truncated 26811 chars]

_source line 1416 · 2026-05-24T18:25:04.692972+00:00_

1	We identify three effectiveness conditions for detect-and-reweight---epoch-matched evaluation, detector calibration alongside discrimination, and minimum intervention over unconstrained maximization---and demonstrate the third via a dual-constrained adaptive $\bstar$ that improves over uniform training at $\rho \geq 0.4$ without per-model tuning, though without measurable downstream gains at 0.6B scale.
2	For practitioners, reweighting is worthwhile only when $\rho \geq 0.4$ and the detector is well-calibrated (Table~\ref{tab:decision}).
3

1	The detect-and-reweight paradigm~\citep{drayson2025detection} has emerged as a response to synthetic contamination in web corpora, now estimated at 20--60\% of crawled data~\citep{cheng2025contamination}.
2	Training on such data risks model collapse~\citep{shumailov2024model,dohmatob2025strong}, motivating methods that downweight likely-synthetic samples via $w(x) = (1 - q(x))^b$, where $q(x)$ is a detector's synthetic probability and $b$ is a sharpening exponent.
3	An alternative, Detect and Remove (D\&R), hard-filters samples above a threshold~\citep{feng2025beyond}.
4	
5	Despite growing adoption, the paradigm's effectiveness conditions are poorly characterized.
6	Does D\&R genuinely outperform soft reweighting, or do published comparisons confound filtering quality with multi-epoch exposure?
7	What detector properties are necessary for effective reweighting?
8	How does the reweighting mechanism behave across contamination levels, and can $b$ be set without per-model grid search?
9	
10	We investigate these questions through controlled experiments on Qwen3-0.6B continued pretraining (132M FineWeb tokens, $\rho{=}0.4$, 5 seeds), with extensions to $\rho \in \{0.2, 0.6\}$ and 1.7B parameters (\S\ref{sec:limitations} discusses scope).
11	To ground the analysis of exponent selection, we implement a dual-constrained formulation as an illustrative application: find the minimum $b$ such that $\aeff(b) \geq \tau$ (quality floor, motivated by convergence theory~\citep{wang2026survive}), subject to $\mathrm{ESS}(b) \geq \nmin$ (stability ceiling)~\citep{kish1965survey,vehtari2024psis}.
12	
13	Our analysis yields three findings:
14	\begin{itemize}[nosep,leftmargin=*]
15	\item \textbf{Epoch confound in D\&R evaluation.}
16	The bulk of D\&R's PPL advantage over adaptive reweighting (${>}$95\%) is attributable to multi-epoch exposure, not filtering quality; epoch-matched controls ($N{=}3$) reduce the gap from 0.396 to $\Delta{=}0.017$ (Table~\ref{tab:epoch_match}).
17	
18	\ite

_source line 1425 · 2026-05-24T18:26:22.337118+00:00_

1	As synthetic text pervades web corpora, detect-and-reweight methods---downweighting likely-synthetic samples during training---have emerged to prevent model collapse, yet when they actually help remains unclear.
2	We investigate this question through controlled continued-pretraining experiments (Qwen3-0.6B, 132M tokens, 5 seeds) and identify three effectiveness conditions.
3	First, published detect-and-remove evaluations are confounded by multi-epoch exposure: over 95\% of the reported advantage vanishes under epoch-matched controls.
4	Second, the detector must provide both sufficient discrimination \emph{and} good calibration; high-AUC detectors with poor calibration degrade performance below uniform training.
5	Third, minimum intervention---applying the smallest reweighting exponent that meets a quality floor---outperforms unconstrained weight maximization.
6	As an illustrative application of the minimum-intervention principle, we derive a dual-constrained adaptive exponent $\bstar$ that improves over uniform training at contamination ratios $\rho \geq 0.4$, reaching conventional significance after multiple-comparison correction, while requiring no per-model tuning.
7	These findings apply within our self-contamination setup at 0.6B scale; generalization to cross-model contamination and larger models remains to be established.
8

1	\paragraph{Synthetic Contamination and Model Collapse.}
2	Training on recursively generated data leads to irreversible quality degradation (\emph{model collapse})~\citep{shumailov2024model}.
3	\citet{alemohammad2024mad} formalize this as Model Autophagy Disorder, showing that quality inevitably degrades without fresh real data.
4	More critically, \citet{dohmatob2025strong} prove under scaling-law regimes that even a vanishingly small synthetic fraction suffices to trigger collapse, ruling out data scaling as a remedy.
5	\citet{cheng2025contamination} confirm that synthetic content now constitutes an estimated 20--60\% of web corpora.
6	Not all synthetic data is harmful: the Phi series~\citep{gunasekar2023textbooks,li2023textbooks2} demonstrates that curated synthetic data can improve training efficiency, but this assumes intentional generation with quality control, unlike the uncontrolled web contamination we address.
7	
8	\paragraph{Detection, Filtering, and Data Reweighting.}
9	Multiple detectors produce continuous scores suitable for downstream weighting: DetectGPT~\citep{mitchell2023detectgpt} uses probability curvature, Binoculars~\citep{hans2024binoculars} cross-perplexity ratios, and Min-$k$\% Prob~\citep{shi2024minkprob} low-probability token statistics; \citet{wu2025survey} survey the broader detection landscape.
10	A parallel line of work optimizes data composition at the domain~\citep{xie2023doremi,xie2023dsir,ye2025mixing} or instance level~\citep{abbas2023semdedup,bengio2009curriculum,mindermann2022prioritized,sow2025dynamic}, using loss, frequency, quality, or domain membership as the selection signal; none targets synthetic provenance.
11	
12	Most directly related to our work, \citet{drayson2025detection} propose $w_j = (1{-}q_j)^b$ for collapse prevention via detect-and-reweight in a from-scratch pretraining setting, where $q_j$ is the detector's synthetic probability.
13	We adopt the same weighting formula but differ in several fundamental ways.
1

103	\subsection{Robustness and Boundary Conditions}
104	\label{sec:robustness}
105	
106	Across three seeds at $\rho = 0.6$, adaptive $\bstar$ ($17.731 \pm 0.004$) outperforms uniform on in-distribution PPL ($17.763 \pm 0.008$, $\Delta{=}{-}0.032$, 3 seeds, paired $t{=}{-}9.95$, $p_{\mathrm{BH}}{=}0.064$), exceeding the $\rho{=}0.4$ improvement ($\Delta{=}{-}0.023$).
107	The ablation hierarchy is preserved (Grid 17.634\footnote{Single seed; full per-seed results in Appendix~\ref{app:rho06}.} ${<}$ Adaptive 17.731 ${<}$ Uniform 17.763 ${\approx}$ ESS-only 17.763 ${<}$ Random 17.861), but the ESS-only/uniform gap collapses to effectively zero (three-seed means both $17.763$) as ESS binding rises to ${\sim}$49\%.
108	Random remains substantially worse than uniform (gap 0.098, $\sigma{=}0.025$): detector signal is load-bearing even when the stability constraint dominates; the adaptive method's continued advantage is driven by the $\aeff$ quality constraint, which operates independently of the ESS ceiling.
109	Three-seed paired comparison confirms $\tau{=}0.7$ outperforms $\tau{=}0.8$ on both holdout and OOD metrics (\S\ref{sec:sensitivity}).

1	\paragraph{The epoch confound in D\&R evaluation.}
2	The epoch confound identified in \S\ref{sec:main-results} accounts for ${>}$95\% of D\&R's reported advantage; the residual ($\Delta{=}0.017$, 3 seeds) falls within cross-seed variance and is robust to LR schedule choice ($\Delta{=}0.006$ under independent schedules).
3	This confound was invisible in \citet{drayson2025detection} because from-scratch pretraining on large corpora rarely completes even one epoch; in continued pretraining, aggressive D\&R filtering (retaining 31.5\% at $q{>}0.3$) creates ${\sim}$3.2 content epochs, making the confound quantitatively significant.
4	A permuted-score control ($+$0.26 vs.\ uniform) confirms that detector signal is load-bearing.
5	
6	\paragraph{When uniform beats reweighting.}
7	Table~\ref{tab:decision} summarizes three conditions, each necessary for effective reweighting: (i)~sufficient detector discrimination \emph{and} calibration (AUC\,$\geq$\,0.9, ECE\,$\leq$\,0.05; \S\ref{sec:robustness}); (ii)~contamination $\rho \geq 0.4$, below which the method degrades ($+$0.046 at $\rho{=}0.2$) with a sharp transition in $[0.3, 0.4]$ (Figure~\ref{fig:delta_vs_rho}, Appendix~\ref{app:rho03}); (iii)~generator--trainee mismatch (at 1.7B the benefit diminishes to $\Delta{=}{-}0.013$ with OOD mixed).
8	When any condition is violated, uniform is the safer default.
